Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any circulation module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 902. The projective resolution has length at most 648 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_902(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 902. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^648(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on circulation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 136. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 459 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 136 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the circulation structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 459. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
